An agent in Main-agent mode may also invoke skills/amw-ascii-creator/ directly via the orchestrator after Phase A approval, applying the full range of diagram types and authoring modes the skill exposes. An agent in Main-agent mode may also invoke skills/amw-html-diagram/ directly via the orchestrator after Phase A approval, applying diagram-editorial and infographics techniques beyond what this command's --style flag exposes. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-create-or-modify-html-diagram.md))

An agent in Main-agent mode may also invoke skills/amw-mermaid-diagram/ directly via the orchestrator after Phase A approval, applying the full grammar selection and annotation techniques the skill exposes beyond this command. Spawns amw-design-md-auditor-agent. Returns a severity-classified findings list (BLOCKER / MAJOR / MINOR / NIT) without modifying the file. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-audit.md))
- **amw-design-md-convert**: Convert a Variant 2 (community 9-section) DESIGN.md into the canonical Variant 1 (official @google/design.md YAML + 8 prose sections) format. Pure-Python — no remote calls, no API key. Runs bin/amw-design-md-convert-v2-to-v1.py. After conversion, the result is automatically lint-checked. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-convert.md))
- **amw-design-md-create**: Author a fresh Variant 1 DESIGN.md from a brief, codebase, URL, or 5-question interactive interview. Spawns amw-design-md-author-agent. The agent assembles YAML frontmatter (colors / typography / rounded / spacing / components) plus the 8 canonical prose sections, runs lint + WCAG contrast checks, and writes the file plus optional companions (tokens.css, tokens.json, component-inventory.md, usage-prompt.md). - **amw-design-md-diff**: Diff two DESIGN.md revisions and surface what tokens / sections changed. Wraps `npx @google/design.md diff` for the structured diff plus a fallback markdown-aware textual diff via bin/amw-design-md-diff.sh. Useful for change-tracking when a brand evolves a token set or when a Tailwind config update propagates new colors. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-diff.md))
- **amw-design-md-emit-companions**: Emit companion files from an existing Variant 1 DESIGN.md — tokens.css (CSS custom properties), tokens.json (W3C Design Tokens), component-inventory.md (per-component variant table), and usage-prompt.md (a copy-paste-ready prompt for downstream agents). - **amw-design-md-from-codebase**: Scan a project tree for Tailwind / shadcn / Chakra / vanilla-CSS / styled-components signals and emit a Variant 1 DESIGN.md whose frontmatter reflects the dominant style system. Pure-Python scanner (no remote calls). Spawns amw-design-md-extractor-agent (input_type=codebase). ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-from-codebase.md))
- **amw-design-md-from-tailwind**: Convert a Tailwind config + globals.css pair into a Variant 1 DESIGN.md. Pure-local Node port of tailwind-to-design-md (no remote calls). Spawns amw-design-md-extractor-agent (input_type=tailwind). ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-from-tailwind.md))
- **amw-design-md-from-url**: Extract a Variant 1 DESIGN.md from a live URL by capturing the page through amw-dev-browser, reading computed styles + DOM landmarks, and emitting canonical YAML frontmatter + prose sections. Spawns amw-design-md-extractor-agent (input_type=url). Faithful transcription only — never invents tokens not present in the source. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-webdesign/blob/HEAD/commands/amw-design-md-from-url.md))
- **amw-design-md-lint**: Validate a DESIGN.md against the official @google/design.md spec — frontmatter shape, section order, token-reference resolution, type rules.
